80b1(phal chen): pas||rgyal sras thams cad sangs rgyas sa lta ste//thams cad sgor yang sangs rgyas mtha' dbung med//rgyal sras kun gyis shes par sla ba min//sangs rgyas mkha' 'dra rnam par 
80b2(phal chen): mi rtog ste//chos nyid mi gnas chos kyi sprin dag gis//sprul pas spyod tshe kun tu rgyas mdzad cing //shing mchog kun drung byang chub 'tshang rgya bstan//sangs rgyas gsung de rab tu 
80b3(phal chen): brjod pas na//chos gnas thams cad ma lus brjod pa yin//'gro ba thams cad re re'i sgo dag nas//sangs rgyas chos rnams kun la rab tu 'dug/de la byang chub sems dpa' 
80b4(phal chen): dag pa dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'ong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/phyogs kyi dbyings thams cad du mngon par byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 'gro ba'i rgyan byed 
80b5(phal chen): pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' yon tan 'od mchog kun tu sgron ma'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems skad cig gis mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i sgo zad mi shes pa ston 
80b6(phal chen): cing||chos kyi dbyings bsam gyis mi khyab pas yongs su sbyin pa dang /rnam par 'dul bar byed pa'i 'od ces bya'o//byang chub sems pa'i kun tu 'bar ba gang gi rgyal mtshan gyi 
80b7(phal chen): rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /zhing yang dag par 'byung ba la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sem dpa' dpal 
81a1(phal chen): @#//phyi 'od zer kun tu bskor 'phro ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//rdzu 'phrul gyi yul gyis rnam par spyod pa don yod pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' yon tan rgya mtsho 
81a2(phal chen): kun tu sgra dbyangs rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan thams cad 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//byang chub soms 
81a3(phal chen): dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul ye shes kun tu snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes su kun nas song zhing chos kyi dbyings la rnam par 
81a4(phal chen): spyod pa'i sprin chen po'i snying po zhes bya'o//byang chub sem dpa' gtsug na rin chen kun tu rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thal ba ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi spyod pa la rab tu zhugs 
81a5(phal chen): shing|byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mtha' yas pa shin tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//byang chub semams dpa' rgyal ba'i gsung kun tu 'jam dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa

81b2(phal chen): pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'ong bas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa rgya mtsho'i phyogs thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe 
81b3(phal chen): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ji snyed gang dag phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing //rgyal bas de kun skad cig dgongs pas sbyangs//'gro bar kun tu zhi ba med

81b5(phal chen): 'gro ba kun lam snying po byang chub tu//byang chub 'tshang rgya rnam par 'phrul ba mdzad//bskal pa brgya phrag stong rnams chad med du//de bzhin gshegs pas sngon 
81b6(phal chen): chad spyod spyad de//nam mkha' 'dra bar byas nas rab bkye zhing //sangs rgyas zhing 'di sngon tshe rab tu sbyangs//mtha' dang dbung med bskal par la la yis//skyo ba 
81b7(phal chen): med par rgyal ba'i yul bsams kyang //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul stobs la mtha' dbung med//phyi mo'i mtha'i bskal ba kun tu 'gengs//rgyal ba'i rdzu 'phrul yul 
82a1(phal chen): @#//la rnam par 'os//phyogs bcu'i zhing rgyan de dag rnam dag pa//rgyal bas 'dir ni kun kyang rab tu bstan//skad cig skad cig sna tshogs gzugs

82a3(phal chen): 'di na rgya chen bsam yas mdzes//sngon tshe'i bskal par rgyal bas 'gro kun tu//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho rgya chen bsnyen bkur bas//de bas 'jig rten gtso la phyogs

82a5(phal chen): yul du mi zad mtha' yas pa'i//bskal pa rgya mtshor byin gyis rlob pa mdzad//sngon tshe'i skal par rgyal bas 'gro don spyad//thugs rje'i rgya mtsho tshad med rab skyed cing //
82a6(phal chen): 'gro ba rgya mtsho kun nas 'khor ba yi//'khor rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas kun kyang sbyangs//sangs rgyas mtshan med sku med rdul dang bral//de bzhin nyid gnas chos 
82a7(phal chen): dbyings snying po ste//'gro ba kun na mtha' yas gzugs su snang //'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal kun zhi mdzad//de la byang chub sems dpa' 'od zer rgya mtsho 'od 
82b1(phal chen): ched dpal gyi zla ba'i rnam par thar ba ni/pha rol tu phyin pa dang sa'i shugs kyis/sems can gyi khams kyi tshul dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag par 'byung ba zhes

82b7(phal chen): ces bya'o||byang chub sems dpa' kun tu ye shes sprin gyi nyi ma'i rgyal mtshan snang ba'i rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes bsgrub par bskal pa mtha' yas pa dag 
83a1(phal chen): @#//chu byin gyis rlob cing rnam par spyod bzhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' mthu stobs brtson 'grus rdo rje yangs pa'i snying po'i rnam par thar pa ni/
83a2(phal chen): chos thams cad kyi phyag rgya mtha' dang dpung med pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//byang chub semasadapa' spos kyi 'od 'phro shin tu 'phar ba'i 'od kyi rgyal mtshan

84a1(phal chen): @#//char yang 'bebs//chos rjes thams cad stobs kyis rnam par dag/bder gshegs ye shes mkha' 'dra mtha' yas te//rgya chen 'gro ba mtha'

84b1(phal chen): khro'i rgyan gyi sgo thams cad nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi/byang chub sems dpa' mang po 'thon par gyur te/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i blo gros

85a1(phal chen): @#//dkyil 'khor gyi sprin thams cad ni mngon par 'byin/yul mtha' yas pa'i spos kun tu 'phro ba'i bdug pa'i sprin ni rnam par 'gyed cing /

85a3(phal chen): 'phro ba kha dog mtha' yas pa'i sgron ma'i sprin gyis ni rab tu snang bar byed/nor bu sna tshogs kyi shing gi yal ga las me tog gi dog pa na rnam par spyod/rin po che'i 
85a4(phal chen): 'od dri ma med pa zad mi shes pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po'i char ni rab tu 'bebs/rgyan mdzes pa thams cad yang dag par ston pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po'i sprin gyi char ni rab tu

85b2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad kyang spyod yul rgya mtsho rgya chen po yongs su dag pa rnams so//sgo kun nas chos kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa rnams so//de bzhin

86a1(phal chen): @#//shes pa'i rgyal po ma/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag

88b5(phal chen): ka ba'i rgyan ni nor bu sna tshogs te//phyogs rnams kun sgor rta babs sprin kyang dres//stegs bu'i phreng ba kun nas rgyan du bkod//gser gyi dril bu'i sprin kyang rnam par brgyas//

115b3(phal chen): da 'byung ste//chos dbyings thugs su chud pas na//'jig rten mgon zhes rab tu brjod//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//'dres pa med cing gnas pa med//sems can thams cad 
115b4(phal chen): yid bzhin du//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu mthong //chos rnams thams cad mngon sangs rgyas//chos rnams kun la rab tu bzhugs//thams cad mkhyen pa thams 
115b5(phal chen): cad gzigs//zhing rnams kun na rnam par mdzes//kha dog bsam gyis mi khyab par//sems can thams cad yid bzhin du//sems can mchog gi 'od rnams dang //

115b7(phal chen): ston//lha rnams re re kun nas kyang //phyogs bcur 'od zer dra ba dag/sangs rgyas gsung ni rab 'byin cing //gdul dka' thams cad rnam par 'dul//sangs rgyas 
116a1(phal chen): @#//'od zer sgo rnams nas//sgra dbyangs zab mo rtag tu 'byung //sangs rgyas yon tan rgya mtsho ni//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul lo//sangs rgyas chos kyi 
116a2(phal chen): 'khor lo dag/mtha' dang dbung med rab tu bskor//ye shes zab mos shes bya ba//rgya chen phyogs su rab tu ston//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad du//rnam par 'phrul 
116a3(phal chen): ba gzhan dang gzhan//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun bkang zhing //yang dag sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//sku rnams re re kun las kyang //zhing rnams kun gyi rdul mnyam pa//

116a5(phal chen): phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos kyi dbyings rnams su kun khyab par//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam par mdzes//rgya chen gsung ni rang 
116a6(phal chen): byung ba//zhing rnams rgya mtshor shin tu sgrog/'jig rten thams cad kun 'jug par//'jig rten mgon po shin tu mdzes//'gro ba kun gyi bsam mkhyen cing //byin gyi rlabs ni 
116a7(phal chen): rab tu sprin//sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun la mngon//sems can thams cad bsam pa bzhin//thams cad gzigs pas sangs rgyas kyi//rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad 
116b1(phal chen): mthang //mtha' yas 'od mnga' sangs rgyas sprin//chos rnams rgya mtsho'i 'brug kyang sgrog/ye shes rnam rgyu'i tshul bzhin du//rgyal ba'i sras rnams de la 'jug/'jug pa 
116b2(phal chen): rnams la 'byung ba med//'byung ba yod par rnam par bstan//gang la sangs rgyas bzhugs pa yi//chos rnams 'byung ba nam mkha' bzhin//'pho dang gnas pa mi mnga' ste//'on kyang 
116b3(phal chen): rgyal ba kun na snang //sangs rgyas chos kyi dbyings las byung //de phyir sangs rgyas rab tu brjod//yul na bzhugs med rang bzhin med//de la 'byung ba mi mnga' ste//sku dang 
116b4(phal chen): mtshan kyang de bzhin med//rgyal ba gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'o//'gro ba kun tu chos kyi sprin//bsam pa kun la rab tu byung //yang dag sangs rgyas rnam kun tu//sems can rgya 
116b5(phal chen): mtsho 'dul ba mdzad//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na snang //'khor gyi rgya mtsho thams cad na//zhing rnams kun gyis rnam par mdzes//

117a1(phal chen): @#//rgyas par//byang chub sems dpa' gang bzhugs pa//de dang de dag sangs rgyas kyi//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul thams cad mthong //ye shes lus kyis zhugs 
117a2(phal chen): pa rnams//zhing kun rdul gyi rlung su yang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang //bdag nyid lus kyang der mthong ngo//gzugs brnyan zhing rnams thams cad dang //sangs 
117a3(phal chen): rgyas kun gyi spyan sngar 'byung //kun tu byin gyis rlabs rnams dang //rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu ston//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang //spyod pa gang gis rnam sbyangs pa//
117a4(phal chen): de dag zhing rnams thams cad du//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnam 'phrul mthong //kun tu mnyam pa nyid gyur cing //kun tu lus kyis rab gnas te//ye shes lus kyi 
117a5(phal chen): rjes song nas//sangs rgyas spyod yul zhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas ye shes zhugs pa'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas kun gyi spu dag tu//

117b2(phal chen): thams cad na//byang chub sems dpa'i sprul pa rnams//sangs rgyas sku la rnam spyod kyang //mtha' ma rnyed par mi rung ngo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' seng ge'i 'od zer snang 
117b3(phal chen): bas mthu 'byin pa'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad kyi//

117b5(phal chen): na gang yod pa//thams cad kun tu 'khor lo 'khor//de ni smon lam rnam 'phrul ba'o//'khor rnams rgya mtsho rgya che ba//gzhan dang gzhan gyi ming dag gis//zhing rnams 
117b6(phal chen): gnas pa kun tu yang //chos kyi 'khor lo kun tu 'khor//rnam par snang mdzad byin gyi rlabs//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun tu yang //kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dag/
117b7(phal chen): rjod cing zhing rnams kun tu 'byung //zhing kun rdul la sku'i rnams//thams cad chos char rab tu 'bebs//'dres pa med cing 'byung ba med//'jig rten dag na rnam par 
118a1(phal chen): @#//mdzes//bskal dbye ba grangs med par//zhing kun rdul phran sbubs dag tu//rnam par snang ba mdzad pa'i gsung //sngon gyi spyod pa rab tu sgrog/zhing rnams

118a3(phal chen): dbyings yongs su rab gsal te//'gro rnams 'dul ba ji bzhin du//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu mthong //dus gsum zhing rnams thams cad kyi//mgon po ji snyed bzhugs 
118a4(phal chen): pa rnams//de dag kun gyi mtshan kyang sgrog/sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul ma ltos//da ltar byung dang 'dag pa dang //sangs rgyas gang rnams ma byon pa//de dag kun gyi

118a7(phal chen): cing||ye shes rtogs pa rnyed pa'i rnams//de dag 'di yi tshul bzhugs//chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam pa'i dbyangs//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu 'byung //sangs rgyas 
118b1(phal chen): kun gyi spyod yul te//byang chub sems dpa'i ched du brjod//skad cig re re'i sems kun la//chos kyi dbyings su rnam par rgyu//de bzhin nyid la gnas shing brten//chos rnams rgya

119a1(phal chen): /byang chub sems dpa' ye shes kyi 'od zer shes rab kun nas sgron ma'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su

119b1(phal chen): 'jig rten kun gyi bsam mthun pas//sems can kun la mngon sum du//lus kyi sprin ni bstan byas shing //byang chub don du sems can 'dul//sangs rgyas thams cad mthu yis 
119b2(phal chen): ni||byang chub sems dpar brjod pa ste//gang gis mgon po mthong ba yi//rgyal ba'i byin rlabs shin tu zab//mgon po ji snyed gang bzhugs pa'i//byin rlabs mtha' 
119b3(phal chen): dang dbung med de//chos dbyings rab tu gang byas nas//byang chub sems dpa' rnam 'tshang rgya//de nas byang chub sems dpa' me tog kun nas 'od 'phro gtsug bud rab tu 'phyang

120a1(phal chen): @#//'dir chu la 'jug par 'gyur//rab tu gnas dang byin rlabs dang //chos kun rnam par spyad pa dang //rab tu 'jug dang ldan pa yi//mnyam pa nyid la 
120a2(phal chen): rab tu zhugs//chos rnams mtha' yas glong gi rnams//chos kun mtshan nyid rnams la te//kun mkhyen nyid la bzhugs shing //chos kyi rgya mtsho kun la spyod//sangs 
120a3(phal chen): rgyas zhing na kun 'khod cing //thams cad kun na rnam par mdzes//'od pa med cing 'phos pa med//sangs rgyas chos nyid de 'dra'o//gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'i sku rnams ni//

120b1(phal chen): kha dog mtha' yas 'bar ba'i 'od//sems can bsam pa kun la 'phro//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na bzhugs//bdud rnams thams cad

120b4(phal chen): spyi'u tshugs dag dang sne la zhi dang //pad+mo'i dbyibs dang khyad khyud dang //dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i khyad par du//gzugs rnams kun gyis rab tu gnas//chos kyi dbyings 
120b5(phal chen): rnams gar phyin par//thug pa med par 'gram 'dal ba//de dag kun gyi 'khor rnams su//shin tu bstan pa'i chos 'byung ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dbyings na//

121a1(phal chen): @#//mtsho'i tshul dag gis//mi zad tshul rnams rab tu bstan//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun na yang //rgyal ba rnams ni mig yer 'drar//snying po byang chub rnams na snang /
121a2(phal chen): 'byung ba dag kyang yod pa min//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings su 'od 'phro ba rnam par snang ba 'byung ba'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par

121b1(phal chen): ni||mos pa rgya mtsho shes pa ste//smon lam kun gyis byung ba'o//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku dang gsung //chags pa med la rab tu zhugs//spyod pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//

122a1(phal chen): @#//mchog phyir kun kyang 'jug/rdul rnams re re'i steng nas kyang //chos kyi dbyings kyi phyogs tshul gyis//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun bkang zhing //sangs rgyas rnam

122a3(phal chen): mi gnas te//nam mkha' bzhin du dngos po med//mnyam pa nyid 'di rtogs gyur nas//ye shes rgya mtsho mthong bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas sku ni ma skyes te//gzung ba med cing byung ba 
122a4(phal chen): med||nam mkha'i mnyam pa nyid dang mtshungs//gzung du med las 'di bstan to//zhing rnams kun gyi sangs rgyas rnams//de dag spu gcig rtse la tshogs//ye shes mig dang

122b1(phal chen): thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa re rer yang /de bzhin gshegs pas rnam par sprul pa'i snying po byang chub dag tu/byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho byed 
122b2(phal chen): pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun nas 'dus par gyur to//'di na ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi snying po byang chub/de bzhin gshegs pas rnal par sprul pa 
122b3(phal chen): dag tu/kun nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag kyang chos nyid 'di kho na rab tu ston to////shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/

137b3(phal chen): la de kun nas 'khyil ba'i dbyibs su 'dug go//'jig rten gyi khams ng+ya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la la ni 'od 'phro ba'i ri'i dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgya 
137b4(phal chen): mtsho 'byam klas pa la la zhing sna tshogs kyi lhun gyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'ji ga rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'abyam klas pa la la ni me tog sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'dug go/
137b5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho 'byam klas pa la la ni khyim sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'jug go/'jig rten gyi khamasargya mtsho 'byam klas pa la ni sems can sna tshogs kyi lus kyi 
137b6(phal chen): dbyibs su 'dug go/'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la lan ni mtshan sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'dag ste/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams kyi dbyibs 
137b7(phal chen): shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'o dbyibs kyi chu la 
138a1(phal chen): @#//rgya mtsho 'di nyid yongs su bstun pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag snang ngo//
138a2(phal chen): rnam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho'i zhing la ltos//rnam phye sna tshogs mtha' yas dbyibs su gnas//dbyibs kyis rnam par phye ba sna tshogs te//phyogs rnams kun na zhing rnams rgya mtsho snang //
138a3(phal chen): la la zlum po la la zur gsum ste//de dag la la phyogs na gru bzhir gnas//nor bu padamo 'khor lo yan lag brgyad//kun kyang las rnams rgya mtshos rnam par bris//rdul med 
138a4(phal chen): 'di kyi dbyibs dang tshul 'dzin cing //gser las grub pa rnam phye sna tshogs te//sgo mang mtha' yas pa nas rgyu zhing 'jug/las rnams rgya mtsho bsam pa dag pas gnas//zhing rnams 
138a5(phal chen): rgya mtsho sna tshogs snang po can//nam par 'di la ji ltar sprin gnas bzhin//de dag rin chen mang gzhi rnam par phye//de dag kun na sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes//zhing rnams 'di kun 
138a6(phal chen): sems kyis rnam par brtags//sna tshogs rgyan dang snang bas kun na gnas//rnam par 'phrul pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//
138a7(phal chen): bde dang sdug bsngal myong bar gyur pa ni//rnam par dag dang nyon mongs tshul bzhin te//chos nyid 'di ni rtag tu yod pa yin//de ltar las rnams rgya mtsho bsam mi khyab//
138b1(phal chen): skra gcig rtse la bsam gyis mi khyab zhing //thams cad rdul snyed sna tshogs dbyibs su gnas//kun na sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad bzhugs//'khor rnams rgya mtsho ches rnams 
138b2(phal chen): rab tu ston//rdul gcig rtse la phra dang yangs pa'i rnams//thams cad rdul snyed kha dog kun dang ldan//ba dog dbyibs rnams kun kyang rnam par brtags//de dag kun na rgyal 
138b3(phal chen): ba'i 'khor lo 'khor//rdul rnams kun la rab tu zhing bstan pa//de dag smon lam rnam par sprul pa yin//nam mkha'i dbyings su las kyi sprul pa ni//sna tshogs sems 
138b4(phal chen): dang bsam pa rnam par brtags//sems can kun mnyam rdul rnams thams cad la//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul pa sprul pa dag/rnam par snang mdzad chos nyid de yin te/
138b5(phal chen): rdul kun la zhing rnams shin tu gnas//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i lhun dag ni gzhan dang gzhan du shes par bya ste/'di ltar 
138b6(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tshol la ni rin po che'i rgyan thams cad kyi lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mkho la la ni rin po che gcig las rnam pa sna tshogs su bkod pa'i 
138b7(phal chen): rgyan gyi lhun can no//'jig rten? gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po che thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi lhun can no//'jig ten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni? 'od 'dogs sna tshogs 
139a1(phal chen): @#//kyi lhan can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rgyan thams cad kyi mdog gi lhun can no//'jig rten kyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rdo rje'i gzhi mi shigs 
139a2(phal chen): pa'i lhun can no//'jig rten gyis khams rgya mtsho la la ni sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul dang byin gyi brlabs kyi lhun can no//'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po che'i mtshan 
139a3(phal chen): nyid kyi lhun can no//'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho la lani sangs rgyas kyis sprul ba'i lhun can no//'jig rton gyi khams rgya mtsho la lan nyi ma'i nor bu'i dkyil 'khor gyi 
139a4(phal chen): lhun can no//'jig rten gi khams rgya mtsho la la ni bon po che shin tu phra ba 'dzin pa'i lhun can no//'jig nyin gyi khams rgya mtsho la lang ni snang ba'i lhun can no//'jig
139a5(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog 'od 'phro ba'i rnam pa'i lhun can no//'jig/rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ba ni dri dang kha dog sna tshogs kyi spos kyi 
139a6(phal chen): lhun can no//'jig rkyen gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni bar po che thams cad kyi me tog gi cod pan gyi lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la yin rin po che thams cad
139a7(phal chen): kyi 'od kyi lhun can no//'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho las ni rgyan thams cad shin tu bstan pa'i lhun can no//'jug rten gyi khams rgya mtshol la ni sems kyi rnam par
139b1(phal chen): rtog pa skad cig gis dmigs pa thams cad yongs su bstan pa'i lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni byang chub sems dpa'i lus kyi rin po che'i dbyibs kyi lhun can no//
139b2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho la la ni rin po che thams cad kyi me tog gi ze ba'i lhun can no//'jig rton gyi khams rgya mtsho la la la ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung dbyangs rnam par 
139b3(phal chen): dmigs pa'i lhun can no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos/yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i lhun shin tu bstan pa rgya mtsho 'di nyid la rab tu gzung bar bya ba'i 
139b4(phal chen): phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la la ni//rin chen rang 
139b5(phal chen): bzhin kun nas gnas//rdo rje bzhin du mi shigs sra//rin chen pad+mo rnams la gnas//la la 'od zer rang bzhin te//dag cing gzud med 'od zer 'byung //rgyan rnams 
139b6(phal chen): kun gyi 'od zer gyis//sna tshogs mkha' bzhin dri ma med//la la 'od kyi rang bzhin te//dag cinga'ado gsal zer la gnas//'od kyi sprin can sna tshogs 'dod//byang chub 
139b7(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams kyi gnas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la ni//mig yor lta bur gnas pa ste//brjod du med cing gzung du med//smon lam dbang gis rab tu byung //la la 
140a1(phal chen): @#//nor bu ma ti bcu/snying po thams cad rnam par 'bar//mu ti gar por lo'i gzhi dag pa//byang chub sems dpas yongs su gang //rin chen 'od 'phro byung 
140a2(phal chen): ba'i zhing //'od 'phro'i sprin gyis mngon khebs shing //rin chen thams cad sna tshogs ldan//thams cad las kyis sprul pa yin//la la mtshan las byung ba ste//gzhi kun 
140a3(phal chen): mtshan gyis kun tu bris//cod pan rnam pa sna tshogs bkod//sangs rgyas kun gyis mngon par sprul//rgya mtsho'i sems las byung ba yi//zhing rnams mos pa nyid la gnas 
140a4(phal chen): yul na mi gnas sgyu ma bzhin//yongs su rtog pas yang dag bskyed//sangs rgyas sku'i 'od zer gyi//nor bus sna tshogs kun tu gnas//sangs rgyas sprin rnams rnam 
140a5(phal chen): par snang //sangs rgyas kun gyi bar rab tu sprul//la la kun tu bzang po yis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho shin tu sprul//nam mkha'i rgyan rnams thams cad dang //smon lam rnams 
140a6(phal chen): kyis rnam par bris//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang //de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba steng du shes par

140b1(phal chen): brjod cing rab tu 'byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi las rnam par smin pa rgya mtsho las rab tu byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di
140b2(phal chen): lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi smon lam rgya mtsho'i glod yongs su sngon pa'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste /dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gzugs brnyan sna tshogs

140b5(phal chen): snying po byang chub kyi rgyan dang /'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyis rnam par ston pa'i rgyan dang /'dilta ste/byang chub sems dba' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang /spyod pa yongs 
140b6(phal chen): su ston pa'i rgyan dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgyan mtsho'i

141a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i rgya che mtha' yas te//rnam 
141a2(phal chen): dag las kyi rnam pa sna tshogs gnas//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnams kun kyang //sna tshogs rgyan can yangs par kun nas gnas//kha dog sna tshogs yangs pa'i

141a7(phal chen): ltos//'das pa'i bskal pa de bzhin ma 'ongs dang //phyogs bcu'i da ltar byung ba ji snyed pa'i//bskal pa de dag rgyan mang yangs pa yang //zhing rnams rgya mtshor re dag na 
141b1(phal chen): snang //dmigs pa kun la sangs rgyas sprin gyi rnams//'gro dang mnyam pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gengs//rnam par 'phrul zhing sems can rnam par 'dul//'di ni zhing rnams rgya 
141b2(phal chen): mtsho'i rnam thar to//spos 'od sprin dang de bzhin me tog klong //rgyan rnams sna tshogs thams cad kun nas kyang //rtag par kun tu khyab par rab tu 'byung //'di ni zhing rnams rgya
141b3(phal chen): mtsho'i rnam thar to//phyogs bcu kun gyi snying po byang chub rnams//sna tshogs rgyan can kun kyang ma lus par//thams cad rgya mtsho'i zhing 'di dbyings dag na//nam mkha'i sprin

141b5(phal chen): pa//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun nas rab tu snang //kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho yongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i

142a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rab tu brtan pa'i yul la rnam par spyod pa dang /byang chub sems/dpa' 
142a2(phal chen): rnams kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /yongs su sbyang ba dang byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi byang chub sems dpa'i sa thams cad kyi 
142a3(phal chen): dbyings la rab tu dgod cing gzung pa'i rnam par spyod pa dang //byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi spyod pa dang /'byung ba rgya mtsho la rnam par spyod pa dang byang chub sems

143a1(phal chen): @#//byas pas//dri med rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams yangs pa 'byung //'byung ba'i spyod pa gang yod tshad med dang //chos rnams thams cad yangs pa yongs sbyangs te//sna

143a7(phal chen): ba la la ni sku tshe'i tshad mi ring ngo//sangs rgyas 'byung ba la la na sku tshe'i tshad ring ngo//sangs rgyas 'gyung ba la la ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing cig yongs su dag par byin gyis rlob po/

144a1(phal chen): @#//dag tu//sems can bsam pa'i phyogs bzhin ston la ltos//sku tshe thung bar la la rnam par ston//ka la la bskal pa bsam yas byin gyis rlob//chos

144a4(phal chen): ston pa mdzad//la la theg pa gcig kyang rab ston te//rgyal ba'i thabs ni gcig kyang mtha' yas so//rang byung la la byang chub sangs rgyas nas//sems can mi mang lam

144a6(phal chen): rgyas sprul pa'i sprin rnams rab tu khyad//'gro ba kun tu de dag mngon bkang zhing //thabs kyi sgo nas sems can 'dul ba mdzad//la la sangs rgyas gsung gis kun bkang
